CREATE TABLE [Exams] (
    [ExamId] int NOT NULL IDENTITY,
    [Title] nvarchar(250) NOT NULL,
    [Description] nvarchar(max) NULL,
    [CourseId] int NULL,
    [DurationMinutes] int NOT NULL,
    [PassingScorePercent] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [IsActive] bit NOT NULL,
    [ShuffleQuestions] bit NOT NULL,
    [ShuffleOptions] bit NOT NULL,
    [CreatedAt] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [LastUpdated] datetime2 NULL,
    CONSTRAINT [PK_Exams] PRIMARY KEY ([ExamId]),
    CONSTRAINT [FK_Exams_Courses_CourseId] FOREIGN KEY ([CourseId]) REFERENCES [Courses] ([CourseId])
);

CREATE TABLE [ExamAttempts] (
    [AttemptId] int NOT NULL IDENTITY,
    [ExamId] int NOT NULL,
    [UserId] int NOT NULL,
    [StartTime] datetime2 NOT NULL,
    [EndTime] datetime2 NULL,
    [Score] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [TotalPoints] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [ScorePercent] decimal(18,2) NOT NULL,
    [IsCompleted] bit NOT NULL,
    [IsPassed] bit NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT [PK_ExamAttempts] PRIMARY KEY ([AttemptId]),
    CONSTRAINT [FK_ExamAttempts_Exams_ExamId] FOREIGN KEY ([ExamId]) REFERENCES [Exams] ([ExamId]) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT [FK_ExamAttempts_Users_UserId] FOREIGN KEY ([UserId]) REFERENCES [Users] ([UserId]) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE [__EFMigrationsHistory] (
        [MigrationId] nvarchar(150) NOT NULL,
        [ProductVersion] nvarchar(32) NOT NULL,

    );
